# Porting the offsets: 155.0.4283.45 -> 155.0.4283.62

Edge auto-updated inside the 155.0.4283 line. `src/edge/offsets.h` was re-pinned with
`tools/re/port_offsets.py` (see `tools/re/README.md`). The `.45` image was already
gone by then, so the port ran from the two public PDBs alone (`EE0411C7...` old,
`29CB6181...` new); nothing below was compared byte for byte against `.45` code.
The `.45` table is recoverable from git history only (`git show` this commit's
parent); the source holds one build.

## Result

| method | entries | how |
|---|---|---|
| `symbol` / `symbol+size` | 232 | the old RVA's symbol name found in the new PDB (an overloaded or folded name picks the candidate of the same size); every one of them has an equal body size in both PDBs where the PDB records one |
| `func+disp` | 2 | a patch site inside a named function: the function's new address plus the old displacement; both land where the disassembly says (see below) |
| `manual` | 1 | `SplitA11yLabel_paneidx_jne`, in cold code that has no symbols |
| removed | 1 | `addr::PA_setup_plus8`: nothing used it |
| unresolved | 0 | |

Not RVAs, and therefore not in the table, but they changed or were checked:

| constant | old | new | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `off::COPILOT_NAME` | 0x46f9 | **0x46dc** | `EdgeCopilotDropdownMenuButton`'s ctor loads 0x46dc, then formats 0x46eb |
| `off::COPILOT_MENU_TITLE_FORMAT` | 0x4708 | **0x46eb** | same call; the old pair was 0xf apart too |
| `off::COPILOT_HIDE_LABEL` | 0x4562 | **0x4545** | `ToolbarMenuButtonContextMenu::AddHideButtonItem` |
| `off::ControlButtons_more_string_id` | 0x4676 | **0x4659** | `ControlButtons::InitButtons` (its close button is 0x465b, 2 above, as before) |
| accessible-label ids in a comment | 0x3e7f/0x3e80 | 0x3e62/0x3e63 | the same cold block |
| `off::COPILOT_HIDE_COMMAND`, `..._SETTINGS_COMMAND`, `page_menu.h` ids | | unchanged | the command ids still sit in `AddHideButtonItem` and the context menu code |
| `off::MENU_icon_color_id` | 0x172 | unchanged | `MenuItemView` still loads it |
| `TabModel` vtable slots 0x18 / 0x108 / 0x118 | | unchanged | read from the running process: GetContents / IsSplit / GetSplit |
| `TSM_collection` 0x10, `TABCOLL_count` 0x18, `TSM_active_tab` 0x98 | | unchanged | `TabStripModel::count`, `GetActiveTab`, `GetWebContentsAt` |

Every string resource id in this build sits 0x1d lower than in `.45` (the five
above, a run of 29 strings removed before them). A string id is a constant of the
resource table, not of the code, so the PDB cannot port it: after an update, check
every `*_string_id` / `COPILOT_*` value by finding its call site (the function
named above is a good start).

## The three patch sites

All three are checked by `verify.h` against the instruction they were reversed
from before the DLL installs anything (the new `PatchSitesHoldTheirInstructions`):

- `SetWebContentsAtIndex_guard_imm` 0xc92c4f2: the `2` of `cmp r8d, 2` (41 83 f8 02)
  in `SetWebContentsAtIndex`, +0xa as before.
- `SetActiveIndex_index_jae` 0xc92c958: `cmp edx, 2; jae` (0F 83), +0xa.
- `SplitA11yLabel_paneidx_jne` 0x42fb7da: no symbol (cold code). Found by its
  shape: `cmp eax, 1; jne <trap>` in the block that maps a pane index to a string
  id, with the trap 0x33c bytes past the jne in both builds. The block has a second
  `cmp eax, 1; jne` at 0x42fb7f3 for the other layout orientation; `.45`'s
  patch covered one site, and so does this port.

## Unverified, or only partly

- The 3-dot menu rows, the dividers' mouse handlers, tab drag (Alt), the starting
  page, link handling and the protocol-handler bubble hook at install time but
  were not driven (they need real input).
- Theme swap with an installed theme: only the default-theme path was applied.
- The enhanced find's `? question` path (needs the assistant's web service).
- Struct layouts: the public PDB has no types, so none could be diffed. A
  struct offset is evidenced only by the functions that read it being the same
  size in both builds and by the live run below.

## Live run (a throwaway Edge, `--user-data-dir` with `edgeplus-port-`, its own LOCALAPPDATA)

Every one of the 43 hooks logged `[ok]`, no `[err]`, the three patches logged the
bytes they replaced (`2`, `0F 85`, `0F 83`). Driven through the control channel and
PrintWindow: a 3-pane and 4-pane split built and tiled, the hover bar with the
grip, closing the extra tabs dissolving the split, Ctrl+F with the native find
bar (`1/1`, the match painted), the chat button switching Copilot <-> Gemini both
ways (`Chat` / `Copilot menu` <-> `Ask Gemini` / `Gemini menu`), and the Gemini
panel opening from the chat button (the Gemini integration has since been
removed; see `docs/re/removed-gemini-integration.md`).

name resolved by the function's size; `symbol+rank` by position among same-named

override. `confidence` is the tool's: `high` = unique name, equal size.
